| [ADR-1118](1118-perceptual-sidedata-weighting.md) | Pelorus side-data perceptually re-weights VMAF **spatial pooling** (opt-in, golden-isolated). The vendored interop parser (ADR-1113) reads each frame's per-cell banding-risk / variance maps; a normalized `[0,1]` salience becomes a per-frame pooling weight `w = 1 + strength·salience`, turning pooled MEAN/HARMONIC_MEAN into their weighted forms (MIN/MAX unaffected). **Golden-gate isolation (load-bearing):** inert unless BOTH the `perceptual_weight` opt-in is set AND a valid Pelorus blob is present for the frame; otherwise `w ≡ 1.0` and the pooling runs the *literal* upstream expression, so the no-side-data path (and the Netflix golden pairs, which carry no side-data) is byte-identical — proven by `test_perceptual_weight.c`. New C-API `vmaf_set_perceptual_{weight_enabled,weight_strength,sidedata}` (`core/include/libvmaf/perceptual_weight.h`); weight module `core/src/feature/perceptual_weight.c`; `vf_libvmaf` reader + `perceptual_weight` AVOption (ffmpeg-patch 0017). R1–R6 compat: `min(known_size,dir.size)` reads, unknown bits ignored, `grid==0` degrades to frame-level scalar, ABI-major mismatch rejected (unweighted + log). CPU-only, no GPU. Alternatives (auxiliary-feature / both) rejected — maintainer chose spatial-pooling weighting. | Accepted | 2026-06-14 | scoring, pooling, pelorus, interop, ffmpeg, golden-gate, fork-local |
| [ADR-1119](1119-golusoris-go-framework-adoption.md) | Adopt the `github.com/golusoris/golusoris` fx framework (pinned `v0.4.0`) across all six vmafx Go binaries + `pkg/`. Every binary becomes an `fx.New(...).Run()` over golusoris modules (Core/otel/HTTP/grpc/k8s-operator/clikit), sharing a vmafx-local `internal/app/bootstrap` stanza (`Base` + `FxLogger`). RC-blocking, phased services-first (`vmafx-server` → `controller` → `node` → `operator`, then `mcp`/`tune` + `pkg` sweep). Keeps the `VMAFX_` config env-prefix via `fx.Replace(config.Options{...})` (not golusoris's default `APP_`) and keeps the controller's embedded SQLite queue (not `golusoris.Jobs`/Postgres). Dependency closures already align byte-for-byte, so the `go.mod` merge is low-risk (`go build ./...` clean post-`go get`). Four framework gaps filed upstream were all integrated by the maintainer (#225 gRPC interceptor injection, #226 version module, #227 operator SetLogger + webhook, #234 log reads `log.level` from config); the pin was bumped v0.3.1→v0.4.0. Only #226 + the `k8s/operator` module are in the v0.4.0 tag; #225/#227/#234 are merged to golusoris main but untagged, so the service binaries carry small interim shims (the #234 log-env bridge; the #227 operator SetLogger + webhook gate) and the controller (needs #225) follows the next golusoris tag. | Accepted | 2026-06-14 | go, framework, fx, golusoris, server, controller, node, operator, mcp, vmaf-tune, rc-blocking, fork-local |
| [ADR-1120](1120-pelorus-abi-minor3-resync-complexity.md) | Re-pin the vendored Pelorus interop ABI (ADR-1113) from `pelorus@835e097` (ABI 1.0) to `pelorus@818d844` (ABI 1.3) and consume the new `PEL_SEC_COMPLEXITY` per-frame section in perceptual weighting (ADR-1118). Minor-3 appends three sections (`QPREPORT` 1.1, `MOTION_CONF` 1.2, `COMPLEXITY` 1.3) and three files; the mirror gains `denoise.h`, `denoise_params.c`, `qp_report_csv.c` (the last is required for the minor-3 conformance fixture to link `pel_x265_csv_parse`). The `PelorusSideData` header layout is unchanged (append-only R1) and the parser keys on `abi_major`, so it is a deliberate re-pin, not a break. Fixes the sync-script `--update` bug that re-vendored the six manifest files but not the conformance-fixture body (the immediately-following drift check then failed). **Complexity modulation:** `perceptual_weight.c` attenuates the banding salience by `(1 − 0.5·complexity)` floored at `0.25` — banding is more visible on flat/simple frames and masked on busy/textured frames. **Golden-isolated (load-bearing):** engages only when the opt-in is set AND a complexity section is present; absent section → factor `1.0` → byte-identical pooling, so the Netflix golden 576×324 pair scores `76.667831` unchanged (verified). Proven by `test_complexity_modulates_weight` / `_grid_zero` (toggle-proven). New vendored files inherit the existing prefix-glob lint/format exclusions. CPU-only, no GPU. Alternatives (trim-fixture, threshold/step modulation, replace-banding) rejected. | Accepted | 2026-06-27 | interop, abi, vendoring, pelorus, scoring, pooling, golden-gate, build, fork-local |
| [ADR-1124](1124-vmafx-tune-go-stage5-per-shot.md) | vmafx-tune-go Stage 5 — `tune-per-shot` port. New `pkg/pershot` (shot ranges, ADR-0513 uniform-window splitter, `vmaf-perShot` JSON/CSV parsers, the `PredicateFn` tuning seam, encoding-plan construction, and a plan-JSON emitter byte-identical to the Python `json.dumps(..., indent=2, sort_keys=True)` output) and `pkg/scorebackend` (per-vendor libvmaf backend probing, strict explicit-request rule per ADR-0667). `pkg/encoder` gains a codec-adapter policy table for the ten codecs `NewExtended` can build — informative vs absolute quality windows (ADR-0538) plus the codec-correct `-c:v` argv shape (ADR-0297 / ADR-0399) — an `AdapterEncoder`, an `EncodeParams.InputArgs` field for pre-input ffmpeg options, and `ProbeSource`. `pkg/bisect` gains `YUVScoreFunc` for raw-YUV-reference scoring. `--predicate-module` and `--fast-nr` are registered but fail fast naming the Python fallback: the first needs a runtime Python import, the second an ONNX Go binding (both Stage 6). Incidentally fixes the Go QSV device-init chain landing after `-c:v` where ffmpeg rejects it (ADR-0601). | Accepted | 2026-08-30 | go, vmaf-tune, migration, codec-adapters, cli, fork-local |
| [ADR-1125](1125-vmafx-tune-go-port-integration.md) | Reconciling seven independently-written vmafx-tune Go ports into one tree. Six packages were invented two or three times over under the same import path; each collision is resolved on evidence rather than merge order. JSON encoding turned out to be the worst case: **four** CPython-JSON implementations (~2,641 LOC) at import paths that never collided in git, measured redundant rather than divergent (200k random float64 -> zero disagreements; 10k renderings match CPython `repr()` and `json.dumps()` exactly), so consolidating them is behaviour-preserving follow-up. Records that the `pkg/codecadapter` tiebreaker — comparing the registries' codec *names* — verified the wrong property: the kept registry had `InvertQuality` inverted for all four VideoToolbox codecs and two argv divergences (ProRes tier 5 `4444xq` vs `xq`, libx265 generic `-pass` vs `-x265-params`), all since fixed and pinned by a per-(codec, preset, quality) argv parity test. Completes the Python CLI shadow: no redirect stubs remain. Integration also surfaced a bare `corpus.jsonl` gitignore pattern excluding a committed fixture, `* text=auto` stripping CRLF from CSV goldens, and a cgo `-L` at a nonexistent path silently linking the distro libvmaf. | Accepted | 2026-08-30 | go, vmaf-tune, python-sunset, integration, fork-local |
| [ADR-1122](1122-vmaf-v1-model-port.md) | Adopt & port **VMAF v1** models (Netflix, libvmaf v3.2.0, June 2026) as an **opt-in** generation while **`vmaf_v0.6.1` stays default**. v1 is still a libsvm ν-SVR fusion but the feature set changed: **VIF removed**, **CAMBI (banding) + `speed_chroma` (chroma) promoted to core**, ADM/motion → `adm3`/`motion3`. Six built-in JSONs (`vmaf_v1.0.16_{3d0h,5d0h,1d5h_2160,3d0h_2160}` + two `_hfr`) for 1080p@3H / phone@5H / 4K@1.5H / 4K@3H (the last on a **[0, 110]** range). Netflix recommends **10-bit** measurement even for 8-bit SDR (banding) — aligns with the zero-copy P010 path (ADR-1121). Port scope: build in the JSONs (`/add-model`); **fix SYCL `cambi`/`speed_chroma`** (now core, currently failing on Arc A380 — fp64 SIGABRT / parity fail); handle `[0,110]` end-to-end (CLI/`--precision`/ffmpeg filters, no clamp-to-100); plumb CAMBI `enc_width/enc_height/enc_bitdepth` (ffmpeg-patch impact); add **new** v1 golden refs in separate tests (the 3 Netflix v0.6.1 golden pairs stay untouched). Alternatives (v1-as-default / don't-port / models-only) rejected — golden-gate + score-scale + broken-SYCL respectively. | Proposed | 2026-07-01 | vmaf, models, upstream-port, cambi, chroma, sycl, golden-gate, fork-local |
| [ADR-1121](1121-sycl-qsv-zerocopy-p010-normalization.md) | Fix `libvmaf_sycl` zero-copy NaN/garbage on QSV-decoded 10-bit pairs. **Two root causes**, neither the orchestration loop blamed by the prior FIX-01 attempt: (1) a **shared QSV/VA surface pool** when both decoders use one `-hwaccel_device` — distinct `mfxFrameSurface1` wrappers map to the same `VASurfaceID`s, so HEVC `ref[N±1]` overwrites the surface AV1 wrote `dis[N]` into (the `sycl_dis[N]==sycl_ref[N±1]` signature); fixed by **requiring separate `-init_hw_device qsv=…` per decoder (FIX-03)**, a documented usage contract, not a code guard. (2) **P010/P012 MSB-aligned pixels** (`V_MSB = V_LSB << (16−bpc)`) copied raw into the shared buffers — the CPU path gets `>>6` for free via FFmpeg's `P010LE→YUV420P10LE`, the SYCL import did not — inflating `integer_motion` 64× and NaN-ing ADM/VIF; fixed by **luma-only `>>(16−bpc)` MSB→LSB normalization (FIX-04)** fused into the Tile4/Y-tiled de-tile store (standalone `launch_p010_normalize()` kernel only on the rare LINEAR/readback fallbacks; no-op for 8-bit) — fusion avoids the per-frame extra full-plane pass that cost ~15% throughput at 4K. A `DMA_BUF_IOCTL_SYNC` flush was tried and **removed** — proven insufficient for the contamination and its blocking fence-wait serialised decode→compute. Also **defaults the zero-copy path to direct dispatch** (`va_import_path` flag on `vmaf_sycl_select_strategy`, after the env overrides): the SYCL combined graph is a net throughput loss on VA-import — byte-identical output but ~15–25% slower at 4K (33→42 fps), so users no longer need the deprecated `VMAF_SYCL_NO_GRAPH=1`; explicit `VMAF_SYCL_USE_GRAPH=1` still wins. D-03 oracle rebased onto the de-contaminated baseline (VMAF 97.2350, motion max 26.6935); SYCL matches to 3 sig figs, 0 NaN. Netflix golden assertions untouched. `VMAF_SYCL_IMPORT_DEBUG=1` diagnostic env knob added. | Accepted | 2026-06-30 | sycl, gpu, ffmpeg, correctness, perf, dispatch, fork-local |
| [ADR-1126](1126-retire-isort-for-ruff.md) | Retires the standalone `isort` pre-commit hook, the `isort` step in the `Python Lint` CI job, and the `[tool.isort]` block in `pyproject.toml`; ruff's `I` ruleset (already selected, already enforcing `I001`) becomes the sole import sorter. The repo ran two independent isort implementations over the same files and, because both auto-fix, `pre-commit run --all-files` never reached a fixed point on `master`: isort rewrote a file and ruff rewrote it back, so the required `Pre-Commit (Formatters + Basic Checks)` check failed with a net-zero `git diff` — which is why it read as a flake. Two genuine behavioural differences, not misconfiguration: (1) for a per-member trailing comment (`from m import (x,  # type: ignore[...])`) isort collapses to one line (89 chars, under the shared 100-char limit) while ruff re-splits to keep the comment attached; (2) given both `from X import (a, b)` and `from X import y as z`, isort orders the alias statement first and ruff second. Setting `combine_as_imports` in both configs was measured and rejected — it churns 8 files and still oscillates, fixing only the second case. Lands as a configuration-only change: `master` is already ruff-clean, verified by three consecutive ruff+black rounds producing an empty `git status`. | Accepted | ci, build, docs, fork-local |
| [ADR-1127](1127-single-semver-release-stream.md) | Replace the fork-suffixed, multi-package release layout with one independent ordinary SemVer stream. The root release owns libvmaf, Python package, Helm appVersion, and node-image pkg-config versions; publishing the release-please draft remains the authenticated fan-out gate. Supersedes ADR-0011. | Superseded by [ADR-1151](1151-vmafx-first-release-1-0-0.md) | 2026-08-31 | release, semver, automation, docs |
| [ADR-1128](1128-fragment-owned-release-cuts.md) | Make changelog fragments the sole release-note source. Release-please skips changelog mutation; a validated pre-merge rollover versions the rendered body, consumes active sources, and records a content-hash receipt. | Accepted | 2026-08-31 | release, changelog, automation, ci, docs |
| [ADR-1129](1129-release-container-runtime-alignment.md) | Bind all release containers to the authenticated published tag, matching nonroot CPU/Python/vendor runtime families, typed MCP 2.x registration, and mandatory per-digest signing, provenance, SBOM, and post-push smoke verification. | Accepted | 2026-08-31 | release, container, supply-chain, security, mcp, go, gpu, ci |
| [ADR-1138](1138-c-translation-units-keep-null.md) | C translation units keep `NULL`; the `modernize-use-nullptr` ratchet stays scoped to C++ TUs via a cited file-scoped NOLINT bracket, because MSVC `/std:clatest` does not document C `nullptr` and upstream-mirror files would re-conflict on every sync. | Accepted | 2026-09-02 | lint, ci, c23, quality-gate, rebase |
| [ADR-1135](1135-ci-twin-drift-gate.md) | Blocking required CI gate that fails when either side of a same-directory .c/.cpp twin pair is compiled by no build file, or when a meson.build / setup.py / *.pyx names a source path that does not exist; known dead twins are allowlisted with a mandatory reason each and stale rows fail. | Accepted | 2026-09-02 | ci, build, process, fork-local, claude-rule |
| [ADR-1137](1137-go-dedup-tune-shadow.md) | One implementation per shared Go package. ADR-1125 left four CPython-JSON encoders (`pkg/pyjson`, `internal/pyjson`, `internal/pyjsonstrict`, `pkg/tune/pyjson`), four copies of `BuildFFmpegCommand` / `ParseVersions`, and a `pkg/tune/` subtree shadowing `pkg/codecadapter`, `pkg/predictor` and the HDR port — already diverged on AMF argv, content-light rounding, coefficient fallback and nil-container rendering. Folded onto `pkg/pyjson` (one reflect-based encoder with a `NonFinite` option, retiring the "deliberately two packages" claim), `pkg/ffencode`, `pkg/codecadapter`, `pkg/predictor`, and `pkg/hdr` / `pkg/pymath` moved out of `pkg/tune/`; per-package names survive as type aliases and one-line wrappers where their own tests pin them, and `pkg/tune/{codec,predictor,pyjson}` stay as one-file transitional aliases until the in-flight sidecar fix (#1187) lands. The ORT-session adapter moves from `cmd/vmafx-tune` into `pkg/predictor`. Every Python-derived fixture travels with the winner, and nine CPython-computed predictor values are pinned as raw bits. Net −2,116 non-test LOC. | Accepted | 2026-09-02 | go, vmafx-tune, python-sunset, refactor, fork-local |
| [ADR-1140](1140-ci-impact-planner.md) | Route required CI work by measured impact instead of pre-declared path filters. Ports `lusoris/k8s` #4964: every workflow hosting an aggregator-required check loses its workflow-level `paths:`/`paths-ignore:` filter and always starts; inside each required job a fail-closed planner (`scripts/ci/plan-ci-impact.py`, driven by `.github/ci-impact.json`) diffs the event's exact revisions (merge-base aware for PRs, exact `before..head` for pushes, NUL-safe name-status with renames) and maps paths onto selectors (`c_core`, `python`, `ai`, `go`, `rust`, `docs`, `shell`, `actions`, `container`) plus closures (`golden_harness`, `tiny_ai`, `python_lint`); heavy steps run only when their selector is impacted, otherwise the job emits a deterministic notice and reports success with the reason. Anything unprovable — unknown top-level path, delete/rename/copy, a CI-authority file, missing merge-base, non-linear push, over-large diff — collapses to `mode=full` (today's behaviour). A doc- or `renovate.json`-only PR now runs the sub-minute gates instead of ~25 min of matrix time; the three divergent `paths-ignore` copies are gone; `scripts/ci/tests/test_ci_impact.py` fails if a top-level path is missing from the map or a required-context workflow regains a path filter. ADR-0313's absent-means-pass rule stays as a backstop only. | Accepted | ci, build, docs, fork-local |
| [ADR-1142](1142-whole-codebase-standards.md) | Every coding standard and lint gate applies to the whole tree — upstream-mirror, vendored, CUDA/HIP/SYCL/Metal kernels, SIMD, tests, tools — with the Netflix golden assertions as the only invariant above the standards. Enforcement moves from ADR-0141's touched-files rule to a whole-tree **ratchet**: `scripts/ci/tidy-ratchet.py` measures every TU of a compile database (diagnostics deduplicated by path/line/column/check, plus NOLINTs without an inline ADR citation) and compares per file against the committed `scripts/ci/tidy-baseline-<lane>.json`; a file above its baseline fails, a cleaned file must tighten the baseline in the same PR, an uncompilable TU fails closed. The `cpu` lane is the aggregator-required context `Clang-Tidy Ratchet (Whole Tree)` and replaces the nightly swallowed scan; `cuda`/`sycl`/`hip` baselines (from the 2026-09-02 workstation measurement) run nightly and via `make tidy-ratchet LANE=…` until hosted toolchains exist. Baselines at landing: cpu 5,241 / cuda 1,650 / sycl 716 / hip 1,173 warnings. | Accepted | ci, process, code-quality, agents, cuda, sycl, hip, metal, simd |
